                format!("expected {}, found {}", expected, found)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::Undefined(name, suggestion) => {
                if let Some(s) = suggestion {
                    format!("'{}' is not defined (did you mean '{}'?)", name, s)
                } else {
                    format!("'{}' is not defined", name)
                }
            }
            TypeErrorKind::UndefinedFunction(name) => {
                format!("function '{}' is not defined", name)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::UndefinedType(name) => {
                format!("type '{}' is not defined", name)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::UndefinedField { type_name, field } => {
                format!("no field '{}' on type '{}'", field, type_name)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::UndefinedMethod { type_name, method } => {
                format!("no method '{}' on type '{}'", method, type_name)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::UndefinedVariant { enum_name, variant } => {
                format!("no variant '{}' in enum '{}'", variant, enum_name)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::InvalidOp(msg) => msg.clone(),
            TypeErrorKind::ArgMismatch { expected, found } => {
                format!("expected {} argument(s), found {}", expected, found)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::InvalidCondition { found } => {
                format!("condition must be Bool, found {}", found)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::InvalidCast { from, to } => {
                format!("cannot cast {} to {}", from, to)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::ReturnTypeMismatch {
                function,
                expected,
                found,
            } => {
                format!("expected {}, found {} in '{}'", expected, found, function)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::UnknownType(name) => {
                format!("unknown type '{}'", name)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::CannotInfer(ctx) => {
                if ctx.is_empty() {
                    "cannot infer type".to_string()
                } else {
                    format!("cannot infer type: {}", ctx)
                }
            }
            TypeErrorKind::Incompatible {
                left,
                right,
                operation,
            } => {
                format!(
                    "incompatible types {} and {} for '{}'",
                    left, right, operation
                )
            }
            TypeErrorKind::CannotConvert { from, to } => {
                format!("cannot convert {} to {}", from, to)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::TupleLengthMismatch { expected, found } => {
                format!("tuple expects {} element(s), found {}", expected, found)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::TypeParamCount { expected, found } => {
                format!("expected {} type parameter(s), found {}", expected, found)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::InvalidArrayElement {
                expected,
                found,
                index,
            } => {
                format!("expected {}, found {} at index {}", expected, found, index)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::InvalidMapKey { found } => {
                format!("map key must be hashable (Str, Int, Bool), found {}", found)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::IfElseMismatch {
                then_type,
                else_type,
            } => {
                format!("expected {}, found {} in else branch", then_type, else_type)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::NilNonOptional { expected } => {
                format!("expected {}, found nil", expected)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::MissingStructField { struct_name, field } => {
                format!("missing field '{}' in struct '{}'", field, struct_name)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::UnknownStructField { struct_name, field } => {
                format!("unknown field '{}' in struct '{}'", field, struct_name)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::InvalidSignature(msg) => msg.clone(),
            TypeErrorKind::RecursiveTypeWithoutBox { type_name } => {
                format!(
                    "recursive type '{}' requires Box to break infinite size",
                    type_name
                )
            }
            TypeErrorKind::MixedTypeArray {
                first,
                found,
                index,
            } => {
                format!(
                    "array element type mismatch: expected {}, found {} at index {}",
                    first, found, index
                )
            }
            TypeErrorKind::MixedTypeMap { kind, first, found } => {
                format!(
                    "map {} type mismatch: expected {}, found {}",
                    kind, first, found
                )
            }
            TypeErrorKind::ConstEvalError(msg) => {
                format!("const evaluation error: {}", msg)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::StaticNotSend { type_name } => {
                format!(
                    "static variable type '{}' must be Send (no borrowed references)",
                    type_name
                )
            }
            TypeErrorKind::UnboundedLoopInConst => {
                "const block contains unbounded loop — must have compile-time-known bound"
                    .to_string()
            }
            TypeErrorKind::ExternInConst { name } => {
                format!("const block cannot call @extern function '{}'", name)
            }
            TypeErrorKind::IoInConst => "const block cannot perform I/O operations".to_string(),
        }
    }
}

/// Kinds of type errors.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum TypeErrorKind {
    Mismatch {
        expected: String,
        found: String,
    },
    Undefined(String, Option<String>),
    UndefinedFunction(String),
    UndefinedType(String),
    UndefinedField {
        type_name: String,
        field: String,
    },
    UndefinedMethod {
        type_name: String,
        method: String,
    },
    UndefinedVariant {
        enum_name: String,
        variant: String,
    },
    InvalidOp(String),
    ArgMismatch {
        expected: usize,
        found: usize,
    },
    InvalidCondition {
        found: String,
    },
    InvalidCast {
        from: String,
        to: String,
    },
    ReturnTypeMismatch {
        function: String,
        expected: String,
        found: String,
    },
    UnknownType(String),
    CannotInfer(String),
    Incompatible {
        left: String,
        right: String,
        operation: String,
    },
    CannotConvert {
        from: String,
        to: String,
    },
    TupleLengthMismatch {
        expected: usize,
        found: usize,
    },
    TypeParamCount {
        expected: usize,
        found: usize,
    },
    InvalidArrayElement {
        expected: String,
        found: String,
        index: usize,
    },
    InvalidMapKey {
        found: String,
    },
    IfElseMismatch {
        then_type: String,
        else_type: String,
    },
    NilNonOptional {
        expected: String,
    },
    MissingStructField {
        struct_name: String,
        field: String,
    },
    UnknownStructField {
        struct_name: String,
        field: String,
    },
    InvalidSignature(String),
    /// Phase 24.3: Recursive type without Box.
    RecursiveTypeWithoutBox {
        type_name: String,
    },
    /// Phase 24.2: Mixed-type array literal.
    MixedTypeArray {
        first: String,
        found: String,
        index: usize,
    },
    /// Phase 24.2: Mixed-type map literal.
    MixedTypeMap {
        kind: String,
        first: String,
        found: String,
    },
    /// Phase 24.4: Const evaluation error.
    ConstEvalError(String),
    /// Phase 24.4: Static type is not Send.
    StaticNotSend {
        type_name: String,
    },
    /// Phase 24.4: Unbounded loop in const block.
    UnboundedLoopInConst,
    /// Phase 24.4: @extern call in const block.
    ExternInConst {
        name: String,
    },
    /// Phase 24.4: I/O in const block.
    IoInConst,
}

/// Type checker that validates THIR type consistency.
///
/// Phase 24: Full type checking on THIR. THIR already carries resolved types
/// (from Phase 22); this pass validates that those types are used correctly
/// in their context.
pub struct TypeChecker<'a> {
    registry: &'a TypeRegistry,
    errors: Vec<TypeError>,
    /// Current function's return type (for return statement validation).
    current_return_type: Option<TypeId>,
    /// Current function's error type (for ? operator validation).
    current_error_type: Option<TypeId>,
    /// Current function name (for error messages).
    current_func_name: String,
    /// Known struct definitions for field checking.
    struct_fields: FxHashMap<String, Vec<(String, TypeId, bool)>>,
    /// Known enum definitions for variant checking.
    enum_variants: FxHashMap<String, Vec<(String, Option<TypeId>)>>,
    /// Whether we're inside a const evaluation context.
    in_const_context: bool,
}

impl<'a> TypeChecker<'a> {
    pub fn new(registry: &'a TypeRegistry) -> Self {
        Self {
            registry,
            errors: Vec::new(),
            current_return_type: None,
            current_error_type: None,
            current_func_name: String::new(),
            struct_fields: FxHashMap::default(),
            enum_variants: FxHashMap::default(),
            in_const_context: false,
        }
    }

    /// Check an entire THIR program for type errors.
    pub fn check_program(&mut self, program: &ThirProgram) -> Result<(), Vec<TypeError>> {
        // Collect struct and enum definitions first
        for item in &program.items {
            match item {
                ThirItem::Struct(s) => self.collect_struct(s),
                ThirItem::Enum(e) => self.collect_enum(e),
                ThirItem::Function(f) => self.collect_function_signature(f),
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        // Check each function body
        for item in &program.items {
            if let ThirItem::Function(func) = item {
                self.check_function(func);
            }
        }

        // Phase 24.3: Validate generic instantiations and recursive types
        for item in &program.items {
            match item {
                ThirItem::Struct(s) => self.validate_recursive_type(s),
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        // Phase 24.4: Check const/static items
        for item in &program.items {
            match item {
                ThirItem::Const(c) => self.check_const(c),
                ThirItem::Static(s) => self.check_static(s),
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        if self.errors.is_empty() {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(self.errors.clone())
        }
    }

    /// Phase 24.2: Type check a single function.
    pub fn check_function(&mut self, func: &ThirFunction) {
        self.current_func_name = func.name.clone();
        self.current_return_type = func.return_type;
        self.current_error_type = func.error_type;

        for stmt in &func.body {
            self.check_stmt(stmt);
        }

        self.current_return_type = None;
        self.current_error_type = None;
        self.current_func_name.clear();
    }

    fn collect_function_signature(&mut self, func: &ThirFunction) {
        // Store function signatures for call site validation
    }

    fn collect_struct(&mut self, s: &ThirStruct) {
        let fields: Vec<(String, TypeId, bool)> = s
            .fields
            .iter()
            .map(|f| (f.name.clone(), f.ty.unwrap_or(builtin::ANY), f.is_public))
            .collect();
        self.struct_fields.insert(s.name.clone(), fields);
    }

    fn collect_enum(&mut self, e: &ThirEnum) {
        let variants: Vec<(String, Option<TypeId>)> = e
            .variants
            .iter()
            .map(|v| (v.name.clone(), v.payload))
            .collect();
        self.enum_variants.insert(e.name.clone(), variants);
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // Statement Type Checking
    // ========================================================================

    fn check_stmt(&mut self, stmt: &ThirStmt) {
        match &stmt.kind {
            ThirStmtKind::Let {
                name,
                ty,
                value,
                mutable: _,
            } => {
                let value_type = self.check_expr(value);
                // Phase 24.2: Assignment — target type must match value type
                if !self.types_compatible(*ty, value_type) {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::Mismatch {
                            expected: self.registry.display_name(*ty),
                            found: self.registry.display_name(value_type),
                        },
                        stmt.span,
                    ));
                }
            }

            ThirStmtKind::Const { name, ty, value } => {
                let value_type = self.check_expr(value);
                if !self.types_compatible(*ty, value_type) {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::Mismatch {
                            expected: self.registry.display_name(*ty),
                            found: self.registry.display_name(value_type),
                        },
                        stmt.span,
                    ));
                }
            }

            ThirStmtKind::TupleLet {
                names,
                type_ids,
                value,
                ..
            } => {
                let value_type = self.check_expr(value);
                // Verify tuple type matches
                if let Some(info) = self.registry.get(value_type) {
                    if let TypeKind::Tuple { elements } = &info.kind {
                        if elements.len() != type_ids.len() {
                            self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                                TypeErrorKind::TupleLengthMismatch {
                                    expected: type_ids.len(),
                                    found: elements.len(),
                                },
                                stmt.span,
                            ));
                        } else {
                            for (i, (expected, actual)) in
                                type_ids.iter().zip(elements.iter()).enumerate()
                            {
                                if !self.types_compatible(*expected, *actual) {
                                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                                        TypeErrorKind::Mismatch {
                                            expected: self.registry.display_name(*expected),
                                            found: self.registry.display_name(*actual),
                                        },
                                        stmt.span,
                                    ));
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                let _ = names;
            }

            ThirStmtKind::Assign { target, value } => {
                let target_type = self.check_expr(target);
                let value_type = self.check_expr(value);
                // Phase 24.2: Assignment — target type must match value type
                if !self.types_compatible(target_type, value_type) {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::Mismatch {
                            expected: self.registry.display_name(target_type),
                            found: self.registry.display_name(value_type),
                        },
                        stmt.span,
                    ));
                }
            }

            ThirStmtKind::Expr(expr) => {
                self.check_expr(expr);
            }

            ThirStmtKind::Return(val) => {
                if let Some(expr) = val {
                    let ret_type = self.check_expr(expr);
                    // Phase 24.2: Return — value type must match function ret type
                    if let Some(expected) = self.current_return_type {
                        if !self.types_compatible(expected, ret_type) {
                            self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                                TypeErrorKind::ReturnTypeMismatch {
                                    function: self.current_func_name.clone(),
                                    expected: self.registry.display_name(expected),
                                    found: self.registry.display_name(ret_type),
                                },
                                stmt.span,
                            ));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            ThirStmtKind::Break(_) | ThirStmtKind::Continue => {}

            ThirStmtKind::While {
                cond,
                body,
                increment,
            } => {
                let cond_type = self.check_expr(cond);
                // Phase 24.2: If condition must be Bool
                if cond_type != builtin::BOOL {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::InvalidCondition {
                            found: self.registry.display_name(cond_type),
                        },
                        stmt.span,
                    ));
                }
                for s in body {
                    self.check_stmt(s);
                }
                for s in increment {
                    self.check_stmt(s);
                }
            }

            ThirStmtKind::Loop { body } => {
                for s in body {
                    self.check_stmt(s);
                }
            }

            ThirStmtKind::Go { expr } => {
                self.check_expr(expr);
            }

            ThirStmtKind::Scope { stmts } => {
                for s in stmts {
                    self.check_stmt(s);
                }
            }

            ThirStmtKind::Drop { ty, .. } => {
                // Validate that the type being dropped is droppable
                let _ = ty;
            }

            ThirStmtKind::ManualErrorExtract { expr, .. } => {
                self.check_expr(expr);
            }
        }
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // Expression Type Checking
    // ========================================================================

    /// Check an expression and return its type.
    fn check_expr(&mut self, expr: &ThirExpr) -> TypeId {
        let ty = expr.ty;

        match &expr.kind {
            ThirExprKind::Literal(lit) => {
                self.check_literal(lit, ty);
            }

            ThirExprKind::Var(name) => {
                // Variable references are resolved in HIR — just validate type exists
                let _ = name;
            }

            ThirExprKind::Binary { op, lhs, rhs } => {
                let lhs_type = self.check_expr(lhs);
                let rhs_type = self.check_expr(rhs);
                self.check_binary_op(*op, lhs_type, rhs_type, ty, expr.span);
            }

            ThirExprKind::Unary { op, expr: operand } => {
                let operand_type = self.check_expr(operand);
                self.check_unary_op(*op, operand_type, ty, expr.span);
            }

            ThirExprKind::Call { func, args } => {
                let func_type = self.check_expr(func);
                for arg in args {
                    self.check_expr(arg);
                }
                // Validate function type has correct arity
                if let Some(info) = self.registry.get(func_type) {
                    if let TypeKind::Function { sig } = &info.kind {
                        if sig.params.len() != args.len() {
                            self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                                TypeErrorKind::ArgMismatch {
                                    expected: sig.params.len(),
                                    found: args.len(),
                                },
                                expr.span,
                            ));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            ThirExprKind::MethodCall {
                receiver,
                method,
                args,
                ..
            } => {
                let recv_type = self.check_expr(receiver);
                for arg in args {
                    self.check_expr(arg);
                }
                // Validate method exists on receiver type
                self.validate_method_call(recv_type, method, args.len(), expr.span);
            }

            ThirExprKind::FieldAccess { object, field, .. } => {
                let obj_type = self.check_expr(object);
                // Validate field exists on object type
                self.validate_field_access(obj_type, field, expr.span);
            }

            ThirExprKind::Index { object, index } => {
                let obj_type = self.check_expr(object);
                let idx_type = self.check_expr(index);
                // Index must be Int for arrays, or match key type for maps
                if let Some(info) = self.registry.get(obj_type) {
                    match &info.kind {
                        TypeKind::Array { .. } => {
                            if idx_type != builtin::INT {
                                self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                                    TypeErrorKind::Mismatch {
                                        expected: "Int".to_string(),
                                        found: self.registry.display_name(idx_type),
                                    },
                                    expr.span,
                                ));
                            }
                        }
                        TypeKind::Map { key, .. } => {
                            if !self.types_compatible(*key, idx_type) {
                                self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                                    TypeErrorKind::Mismatch {
                                        expected: self.registry.display_name(*key),
                                        found: self.registry.display_name(idx_type),
                                    },
                                    expr.span,
                                ));
                            }
                        }
                        _ => {}
                    }
                }
            }

            ThirExprKind::ArrayLiteral(elements) => {
                // Phase 24.2: All array elements must have same type
                if !elements.is_empty() {
                    let first_type = self.check_expr(&elements[0]);
                    for (i, elem) in elements.iter().enumerate().skip(1) {
                        let elem_type = self.check_expr(elem);
                        if !self.types_compatible(first_type, elem_type) {
                            self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                                TypeErrorKind::MixedTypeArray {
                                    first: self.registry.display_name(first_type),
                                    found: self.registry.display_name(elem_type),
                                    index: i,
                                },
                                expr.span,
                            ));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            ThirExprKind::MapLiteral(entries) => {
                // Phase 24.2: All keys same type, all values same type
                if !entries.is_empty() {
                    let first_key_type = self.check_expr(&entries[0].0);
                    let first_val_type = self.check_expr(&entries[0].1);
                    for (k, v) in entries.iter().skip(1) {
                        let key_type = self.check_expr(k);
                        let val_type = self.check_expr(v);
                        if !self.types_compatible(first_key_type, key_type) {
                            self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                                TypeErrorKind::MixedTypeMap {
                                    kind: "key".to_string(),
                                    first: self.registry.display_name(first_key_type),
                                    found: self.registry.display_name(key_type),
                                },
                                expr.span,
                            ));
                        }
                        if !self.types_compatible(first_val_type, val_type) {
                            self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                                TypeErrorKind::MixedTypeMap {
                                    kind: "value".to_string(),
                                    first: self.registry.display_name(first_val_type),
                                    found: self.registry.display_name(val_type),
                                },
                                expr.span,
                            ));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            ThirExprKind::StructLiteral { name, fields } => {
                // Validate all required fields are present
                if let Some(struct_fields) = self.struct_fields.get(name) {
                    for (field_name, _, _) in struct_fields {
                        let found = fields.iter().any(|(n, _)| n == field_name);
                        if !found {
                            self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                                TypeErrorKind::MissingStructField {
                                    struct_name: name.clone(),
                                    field: field_name.clone(),
                                },
                                expr.span,
                            ));
                        }
                    }
                    // Check for unknown fields
                    for (field_name, _) in fields {
                        let known = struct_fields.iter().any(|(n, _, _)| n == field_name);
                        if !known {
                            self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                                TypeErrorKind::UnknownStructField {
                                    struct_name: name.clone(),
                                    field: field_name.clone(),
                                },
                                expr.span,
                            ));
                        }
                    }
                }
                for (_, value) in fields {
                    self.check_expr(value);
                }
            }

            ThirExprKind::EnumVariant {
                enum_name,
                variant,
                payload,
            } => {
                // Validate variant exists in enum
                if let Some(variants) = self.enum_variants.get(enum_name) {
                    let found = variants.iter().find(|(n, _)| n == variant);
                    if found.is_none() {
                        self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                            TypeErrorKind::UndefinedVariant {
                                enum_name: enum_name.clone(),
                                variant: variant.clone(),
                            },
                            expr.span,
                        ));
                    }
                }
                for p in payload {
                    self.check_expr(p);
                }
            }

            ThirExprKind::Tuple(elements) => {
                for elem in elements {
                    self.check_expr(elem);
                }
            }

            ThirExprKind::Spread(inner) => {
                self.check_expr(inner);
            }

            ThirExprKind::If { cond, then, else_ } => {
                let cond_type = self.check_expr(cond);
                // Phase 24.2: If condition must be Bool
                if cond_type != builtin::BOOL {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::InvalidCondition {
                            found: self.registry.display_name(cond_type),
                        },
                        expr.span,
                    ));
                }
                let then_type = self.check_expr(then);
                if let Some(else_expr) = else_ {
                    let else_type = self.check_expr(else_expr);
                    // Phase 24.2: Match arms (if/else) must have same type
                    if !self.types_compatible(then_type, else_type) {
                        self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                            TypeErrorKind::IfElseMismatch {
                                then_type: self.registry.display_name(then_type),
                                else_type: self.registry.display_name(else_type),
                            },
                            expr.span,
                        ));
                    }
                }
            }

            ThirExprKind::Match {
                expr: scrutinee,
                arms,
            } => {
                let _scrutinee_type = self.check_expr(scrutinee);
                // Phase 24.2: All match arms must have same type
                if !arms.is_empty() {
                    let first_arm_type = self.check_expr(&arms[0].body);
                    for arm in arms.iter().skip(1) {
                        if let Some(guard) = &arm.guard {
                            self.check_expr(guard);
                        }
                        let arm_type = self.check_expr(&arm.body);
                        if !self.types_compatible(first_arm_type, arm_type) {
                            self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                                TypeErrorKind::Mismatch {
                                    expected: self.registry.display_name(first_arm_type),
                                    found: self.registry.display_name(arm_type),
                                },
                                expr.span,
                            ));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            ThirExprKind::Block(stmts, tail) => {
                for s in stmts {
                    self.check_stmt(s);
                }
                if let Some(e) = tail {
                    self.check_expr(e);
                }
            }

            ThirExprKind::Range { start, end, .. } => {
                if let Some(s) = start {
                    self.check_expr(s);
                }
                if let Some(e) = end {
                    self.check_expr(e);
                }
            }

            // Error handling expressions
            ThirExprKind::Ok(inner) => {
                self.check_expr(inner);
            }
            ThirExprKind::Err(inner) => {
                self.check_expr(inner);
            }
            ThirExprKind::Try(inner) => {
                // Phase 25.2: ? operator — must be in function returning Result
                let inner_type = self.check_expr(inner);
                if let Some(info) = self.registry.get(inner_type) {
                    if !matches!(info.kind, TypeKind::Result { .. }) {
                        self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                            TypeErrorKind::InvalidOp(
                                "`?` operator can only be used on Result types".to_string(),
                            ),
                            expr.span,
                        ));
                    }
                }
            }
            ThirExprKind::UnwrapOrPanic {
                expr: inner,
                message,
            } => {
                self.check_expr(inner);
                self.check_expr(message);
            }

            // Ownership annotations
            ThirExprKind::Move(inner) => {
                self.check_expr(inner);
            }
            ThirExprKind::Borrow { expr: inner, .. } => {
                self.check_expr(inner);
            }
            ThirExprKind::Clone(inner) => {
                self.check_expr(inner);
            }

            // Closures
            ThirExprKind::Closure { body, .. } => {
                self.check_expr(body);
            }

            // Cast
            ThirExprKind::Cast { value, to_type } => {
                let from_type = self.check_expr(value);
                // Phase 24.2: Validate cast is legal
                if !self.is_valid_cast(from_type, *to_type) {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::InvalidCast {
                            from: self.registry.display_name(from_type),
                            to: self.registry.display_name(*to_type),
                        },
                        expr.span,
                    ));
                }
            }

            // Async
            ThirExprKind::Async(inner) => {
                self.check_expr(inner);
            }
            ThirExprKind::Await(inner) => {
                self.check_expr(inner);
            }
            ThirExprKind::Spawn(inner) => {
                self.check_expr(inner);
            }
            ThirExprKind::ScopeBlock { stmts } => {
                for s in stmts {
                    self.check_stmt(s);
                }
            }
        }

        ty
    }

    fn check_literal(&mut self, lit: &ThirLiteral, ty: TypeId) {
        match lit {
            ThirLiteral::Int(_) => {
                // Int literals can be any integer type
            }
            ThirLiteral::Float(_) => {
                // Float literals can be any float type
            }
            ThirLiteral::String(_) => {
                if ty != builtin::STR {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::Mismatch {
                            expected: self.registry.display_name(ty),
                            found: "Str".to_string(),
                        },
                        Span::dummy(),
                    ));
                }
            }
            ThirLiteral::Bool(_) => {
                if ty != builtin::BOOL {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::Mismatch {
                            expected: self.registry.display_name(ty),
                            found: "Bool".to_string(),
                        },
                        Span::dummy(),
                    ));
                }
            }
            ThirLiteral::Null => {
                // Null is only valid for Optional types
                if let Some(info) = self.registry.get(ty) {
                    if !matches!(info.kind, TypeKind::Optional { .. } | TypeKind::Any) {
                        self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                            TypeErrorKind::NilNonOptional {
                                expected: self.registry.display_name(ty),
                            },
                            Span::dummy(),
                        ));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fn check_binary_op(
        &mut self,
        op: ThirBinOp,
        lhs: TypeId,
        rhs: TypeId,
        result: TypeId,
        span: Span,
    ) {
        use doo_thir::ThirBinOp::*;
        match op {
            Add | Sub | Mul | Div | Mod => {
                // Arithmetic: both operands must be numeric (Int or Float)
                if !self.is_numeric(lhs) || !self.is_numeric(rhs) {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::Incompatible {
                            left: self.registry.display_name(lhs),
                            right: self.registry.display_name(rhs),
                            operation: format!("{:?}", op),
                        },
                        span,
                    ));
                }
            }
            Eq | NotEq | Lt | Gt | LtEq | GtEq => {
                // Comparison: both operands must be same type
                if !self.types_compatible(lhs, rhs) {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::Incompatible {
                            left: self.registry.display_name(lhs),
                            right: self.registry.display_name(rhs),
                            operation: format!("{:?}", op),
                        },
                        span,
                    ));
                }
                // Result must be Bool
                if result != builtin::BOOL {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::Mismatch {
                            expected: "Bool".to_string(),
                            found: self.registry.display_name(result),
                        },
                        span,
                    ));
                }
            }
            And | Or => {
                // Logical: both operands must be Bool
                if lhs != builtin::BOOL || rhs != builtin::BOOL {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::Incompatible {
                            left: self.registry.display_name(lhs),
                            right: self.registry.display_name(rhs),
                            operation: format!("{:?}", op),
                        },
                        span,
                    ));
                }
            }
            BitAnd | BitOr | BitXor => {
                // Bitwise: both operands must be Int or Bool
                if !self.is_numeric(lhs) && lhs != builtin::BOOL {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::InvalidOp(format!(
                            "bitwise operation requires Int or Bool, found {}",
                            self.registry.display_name(lhs)
                        )),
                        span,
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fn check_unary_op(&mut self, op: ThirUnOp, operand: TypeId, _result: TypeId, span: Span) {
        use doo_thir::ThirUnOp::*;
        match op {
            Neg => {
                if !self.is_numeric(operand) {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::InvalidOp(format!(
                            "negation requires numeric type, found {}",
                            self.registry.display_name(operand)
                        )),
                        span,
                    ));
                }
            }
            Not => {
                if operand != builtin::BOOL && !self.is_numeric(operand) {
                    self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                        TypeErrorKind::InvalidOp(format!(
                            "not requires Bool or Int, found {}",
                            self.registry.display_name(operand)
                        )),
                        span,
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // Phase 24.1: Type Compatibility
    // ========================================================================

    /// Check if two types are compatible.
    ///
    /// Phase 24.1:
    /// - Same primitive → compatible
    /// - Same named type with compatible args → compatible
    /// - Function types: contravariant params, covariant ret
    /// - Option/Result: compatible if inner types compatible
    /// - NO implicit numeric conversion (MMM Part II §2)
    /// - NO mixed-type collections (MMM Part V §2)
    pub fn types_compatible(&self, a: TypeId, b: TypeId) -> bool {
        if a == b {
            return true;
        }

        // Any is compatible with everything
        if a == builtin::ANY || b == builtin::ANY {
            return true;
        }

        let a_info = match self.registry.get(a) {
            Some(i) => i,
            None => return false,
        };
        let b_info = match self.registry.get(b) {
            Some(i) => i,
            None => return false,
        };

        // Optional compatibility: T is assignable to T?
        match (&a_info.kind, &b_info.kind) {
            (TypeKind::Optional { inner }, _) => {
                return self.types_compatible(*inner, b);
            }
            (_, TypeKind::Optional { inner }) => {
                return self.types_compatible(a, *inner);
            }
            _ => {}
        }

        // Result compatibility: inner types must match
        match (&a_info.kind, &b_info.kind) {
            (
                TypeKind::Result {
                    ok: ok_a,
                    err: err_a,
                },
                TypeKind::Result {
                    ok: ok_b,
                    err: err_b,
                },
            ) => {
                return self.types_compatible(*ok_a, *ok_b)
                    && self.types_compatible(*err_a, *err_b);
            }
            _ => {}
        }

        // Array compatibility: element types must match
        match (&a_info.kind, &b_info.kind) {
            (TypeKind::Array { element: elem_a }, TypeKind::Array { element: elem_b }) => {
                return self.types_compatible(*elem_a, *elem_b);
            }
            _ => {}
        }

        // Map compatibility: key and value types must match
        match (&a_info.kind, &b_info.kind) {
            (
                TypeKind::Map {
                    key: k_a,
                    value: v_a,
                },
                TypeKind::Map {
                    key: k_b,
                    value: v_b,
                },
            ) => {
                return self.types_compatible(*k_a, *k_b) && self.types_compatible(*v_a, *v_b);
            }
            _ => {}
        }

        // Tuple compatibility: same length, each element compatible
        match (&a_info.kind, &b_info.kind) {
            (TypeKind::Tuple { elements: elems_a }, TypeKind::Tuple { elements: elems_b }) => {
                if elems_a.len() != elems_b.len() {
                    return false;
                }
                return elems_a
                    .iter()
                    .zip(elems_b.iter())
                    .all(|(a, b)| self.types_compatible(*a, *b));
            }
            _ => {}
        }

        // Function type compatibility: contravariant params, covariant return
        match (&a_info.kind, &b_info.kind) {
            (TypeKind::Function { sig: sig_a }, TypeKind::Function { sig: sig_b }) => {
                if sig_a.params.len() != sig_b.params.len() {
                    return false;
                }
                // Contravariant: params of b must be compatible with params of a
                let params_ok = sig_a
                    .params
                    .iter()
                    .zip(sig_b.params.iter())
                    .all(|(pa, pb)| self.types_compatible(*pb, *pa));
                // Covariant: return of a must be compatible with return of b
                let ret_ok = self.types_compatible(sig_a.return_type, sig_b.return_type);
                return params_ok && ret_ok;
            }
            _ => {}
        }

        // Struct/Enum: same name
        match (&a_info.kind, &b_info.kind) {
            (TypeKind::Struct { def: def_a }, TypeKind::Struct { def: def_b }) => {
                return def_a.name == def_b.name;
            }
            (TypeKind::Enum { def: def_a }, TypeKind::Enum { def: def_b }) => {
                return def_a.name == def_b.name;
            }
            _ => {}
        }

        // Same name → compatible (for named types)
        if !a_info.name.is_empty() && a_info.name == b_info.name {
            return true;
        }

        false
    }

    /// Check if a type is numeric (Int variants or Float variants).
    fn is_numeric(&self, ty: TypeId) -> bool {
        if let Some(info) = self.registry.get(ty) {
            matches!(
                info.kind,
                TypeKind::Bool
                    | TypeKind::Char
                    | TypeKind::Int8
                    | TypeKind::Int16
                    | TypeKind::Int32
                    | TypeKind::Int64
                    | TypeKind::Int
                    | TypeKind::UInt8
                    | TypeKind::UInt16
                    | TypeKind::UInt32
                    | TypeKind::UInt64
                    | TypeKind::UInt
                    | TypeKind::Float32
                    | TypeKind::Float64
            )
        } else {
            false
        }
    }

    /// Check if a cast from one type to another is valid.
    fn is_valid_cast(&self, from: TypeId, to: TypeId) -> bool {
        if from == to {
            return true;
        }
        // Numeric casts: Int <-> Float, Int sizes <-> Int
        if self.is_numeric(from) && self.is_numeric(to) {
            return true;
        }
        // Any cast
        if from == builtin::ANY || to == builtin::ANY {
            return true;
        }
        false
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // Phase 24.3: Generic Instantiation / Recursive Type Validation
    // ========================================================================

    /// Validate that recursive types use Box to break infinite size.
    /// MMM Part II §5: struct Node { next: Node } → error: missing Box
    fn validate_recursive_type(&mut self, s: &ThirStruct) {
        for field in &s.fields {
            if let Some(field_ty) = field.ty {
                if self.is_directly_recursive(field_ty, &s.name) {
                    // Check if the field type is Box
                    let is_boxed = self
                        .registry
                        .get(field_ty)
                        .map(|info| matches!(info.kind, TypeKind::Box { .. }))
                        .unwrap_or(false);

                    if !is_boxed {
                        self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                            TypeErrorKind::RecursiveTypeWithoutBox {
                                type_name: s.name.clone(),
                            },
                            field.span,
                        ));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Check if a type directly references the given struct name (recursive).
    fn is_directly_recursive(&self, ty: TypeId, struct_name: &str) -> bool {
        if let Some(info) = self.registry.get(ty) {
            if let TypeKind::Struct { def } = &info.kind {
                return def.name.resolve() == struct_name;
            }
            // Check if it's a TypeRef to this struct
            if let TypeKind::TypeRef { name } = &info.kind {
                return name.resolve() == struct_name;
            }
        }
        false
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // Phase 24.4: Const/Static Type Checking
    // ========================================================================

    /// Check a const declaration.
    /// Phase 24.4: const values must have compile-time-known type.
    /// CTFE validation: no I/O, no @extern, no unbounded loops.
    fn check_const(&mut self, c: &ThirConst) {
        let prev_const = self.in_const_context;
        self.in_const_context = true;

        // Check the value expression
        let value_type = self.check_expr(&c.value_expr);

        // Validate type matches declaration
        if !self.types_compatible(c.ty, value_type) {
            self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                TypeErrorKind::Mismatch {
                    expected: self.registry.display_name(c.ty),
                    found: self.registry.display_name(value_type),
                },
                c.span,
            ));
        }

        // CTFE validation: check for forbidden operations in const context
        self.validate_const_expr(&c.value_expr);

        self.in_const_context = prev_const;
    }

    /// Validate that a const expression doesn't contain forbidden operations.
    /// Phase 24.4 / DOO_LET §3: No I/O, no @extern, no unbounded loops.
    fn validate_const_expr(&mut self, expr: &ThirExpr) {
        match &expr.kind {
            // Check for calls — must not be @extern or I/O
            ThirExprKind::Call { func, .. } => {
                if let ThirExprKind::Var(name) = &func.kind {
                    // Flag extern calls in const context
                    if name.starts_with("doo_") || name.starts_with("__ffi_") {
                        self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                            TypeErrorKind::ExternInConst { name: name.clone() },
                            expr.span,
                        ));
                    }
                }
            }
            // Recurse into sub-expressions
            ThirExprKind::Binary { lhs, rhs, .. } => {
                self.validate_const_expr(lhs);
                self.validate_const_expr(rhs);
            }
            ThirExprKind::Unary { expr: inner, .. } => {
                self.validate_const_expr(inner);
            }
            ThirExprKind::Block(stmts, tail) => {
                for s in stmts {
                    if let ThirStmtKind::While { .. } | ThirStmtKind::Loop { .. } = &s.kind {
                        // Loops in const must have compile-time-known bounds
                        // For now, flag all loops as potentially unbounded
                        self.errors
                            .push(TypeError::new(TypeErrorKind::UnboundedLoopInConst, s.span));
                    }
                }
                if let Some(e) = tail {
                    self.validate_const_expr(e);
                }
            }
            ThirExprKind::If { cond, then, else_ } => {
                self.validate_const_expr(cond);
                self.validate_const_expr(then);
                if let Some(e) = else_ {
                    self.validate_const_expr(e);
                }
            }
            ThirExprKind::ArrayLiteral(elements) => {
                for e in elements {
                    self.validate_const_expr(e);
                }
            }
            ThirExprKind::MapLiteral(entries) => {
                for (k, v) in entries {
                    self.validate_const_expr(k);
                    self.validate_const_expr(v);
                }
            }
            ThirExprKind::StructLiteral { fields, .. } => {
                for (_, v) in fields {
                    self.validate_const_expr(v);
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    /// Check a static declaration.
    /// Phase 24.4: static values must be Send-equivalent (no borrowed references).
    fn check_static(&mut self, s: &doo_thir::ThirStatic) {
        if let Some(ty) = s.ty {
            if !self.is_send_type(ty) {
                self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                    TypeErrorKind::StaticNotSend {
                        type_name: self.registry.display_name(ty),
                    },
                    s.span,
                ));
            }
        }
    }

    /// Check if a type is Send (no borrowed references).
    /// Simplified: all Doo types are Send by default since there are no
    /// borrowed references in the surface language.
    fn is_send_type(&self, ty: TypeId) -> bool {
        if let Some(info) = self.registry.get(ty) {
            match &info.kind {
                // All Doo types are Send — no & references in surface language
                _ => true,
            }
        } else {
            true
        }
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // Validation Helpers
    // ========================================================================

    fn validate_method_call(
        &mut self,
        recv_type: TypeId,
        method: &str,
        arg_count: usize,
        span: Span,
    ) {
        // Check if method is a known builtin method
        if doo_core::methods::is_builtin_method(method) {
            return;
        }

        // Check struct methods
        if let Some(info) = self.registry.get(recv_type) {
            if let TypeKind::Struct { def } = &info.kind {
                let type_name = def.name.resolve();
                // User-defined methods are resolved in THIR — just validate they exist
                // The MethodResolver handles this; we skip here to avoid false positives
                let _ = type_name;
            }
        }

        let _ = (arg_count, span);
    }

    fn validate_field_access(&mut self, obj_type: TypeId, field: &str, span: Span) {
        if let Some(info) = self.registry.get(obj_type) {
            if let TypeKind::Struct { def } = &info.kind {
                let type_name = def.name.resolve();
                if let Some(fields) = self.struct_fields.get(type_name) {
                    let found = fields.iter().any(|(n, _, _)| n == field);
                    if !found {
                        self.errors.push(TypeError::new(
                            TypeErrorKind::UndefinedField {
                                type_name: type_name.to_string(),
                                field: field.to_string(),
                            },
                            span,
                        ));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Take collected errors.
    pub fn take_errors(&mut self) -> Vec<TypeError> {
        std::mem::take(&mut self.errors)
    }

    /// Check if any errors were collected.
    pub fn has_errors(&self) -> bool {
        !self.errors.is_empty()
    }
}

// Re-export ThirBinOp and ThirUnOp for convenience
pub use doo_thir::{ThirBinOp, ThirUnOp};

// ============================================================================
// Tests
// ============================================================================

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_type_checker_creation() {
        let registry = TypeRegistry::new();
        let checker = TypeChecker::new(&registry);
        assert!(!checker.has_errors());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_types_compatible_same() {
        let registry = TypeRegistry::new();
        let checker = TypeChecker::new(&registry);
        assert!(checker.types_compatible(builtin::INT, builtin::INT));
        assert!(checker.types_compatible(builtin::STR, builtin::STR));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_types_compatible_any() {
        let registry = TypeRegistry::new();
        let checker = TypeChecker::new(&registry);
        assert!(checker.types_compatible(builtin::INT, builtin::ANY));
        assert!(checker.types_compatible(builtin::ANY, builtin::STR));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_types_compatible_no_implicit_numeric() {
        // Phase 24.1: NO implicit numeric conversion (MMM Part II §2)
        let registry = TypeRegistry::new();
        let checker = TypeChecker::new(&registry);
        // Int and Float are NOT compatible without explicit cast
        assert!(!checker.types_compatible(builtin::INT, builtin::FLOAT));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_is_numeric() {
        let registry = TypeRegistry::new();
        let checker = TypeChecker::new(&registry);
        assert!(checker.is_numeric(builtin::INT));
        assert!(checker.is_numeric(builtin::FLOAT));
        assert!(checker.is_numeric(builtin::BOOL));
        assert!(!checker.is_numeric(builtin::STR));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_is_valid_cast() {
        let registry = TypeRegistry::new();
        let checker = TypeChecker::new(&registry);
        assert!(checker.is_valid_cast(builtin::INT, builtin::FLOAT));
        assert!(checker.is_valid_cast(builtin::INT, builtin::INT));
        assert!(!checker.is_valid_cast(builtin::STR, builtin::INT));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_type_error_message() {
        let err = TypeError::new(
            TypeErrorKind::Mismatch {
                expected: "Int".to_string(),
                found: "Str".to_string(),
            },
            Span::dummy(),
        );
        assert!(err.message().contains("expected Int"));
        assert!(err.message().contains("found Str"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_recursive_type_error() {
        let err = TypeError::new(
            TypeErrorKind::RecursiveTypeWithoutBox {
                type_name: "Node".to_string(),
            },
            Span::dummy(),
        );
        assert!(err.message().contains("Node"));
        assert!(err.message().contains("Box"));
    }
}
